(typeof input.error !== \"object\" || input.error === null) return undefined;\n  try {\n    const code = Reflect.get(input.error, \"code\");\n    return typeof code === \"string\" ? code : undefined;\n  } catch {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function isCorruptStorageError(input: { error: unknown }): boolean {\n  const code = storageErrorCode(input);\n  return code === \"SQLITE_CORRUPT\" || code === \"SQLITE_NOTADB\";\n}\n\nconst unavailableExactCodes = new Set([\"EACCES\", \"EPERM\", \"EBUSY\"]);\nconst unavailableSqliteCodeFamilies = [\n  \"SQLITE_AUTH\",\n  \"SQLITE_BUSY\",\n  \"SQLITE_CANTOPEN\",\n  \"SQLITE_IOERR\",\n  \"SQLITE_LOCKED\",\n  \"SQLITE_PERM\",\n  \"SQLITE_READONLY\",\n] as const;\n\nfunction belongsToErrorCodeFamily(code: string | subclasses included. unknown;\n  message: string;\n}): ProjectStorageBrokenError | ProjectStorageUnavailableError {\n  if (isKnownStorageFailure(input.error)) return input.error;\n  if (isUnavailableStorageError({ error: input.error })) {\n    return new ProjectStorageUnavailableError(\"Project Storage authority is unavailable.\");\n  }\n  return new ProjectStorageBrokenError(input.message, { cause: input.error exactly once.\");\n}\n\nasync function vacuumInto(sourcePath: string, targetPath: string): Promise<void> {\n  await withDatabase(sourcePath, { readOnly: true }, async (client) => {\n    await
